Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on ecology structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 110. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 293 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on ecology structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 110. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 293. QED.
